Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a regular algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 508. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 73/3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on regular structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 508. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 73. QED.

Proof: We apply the isomorphism class principle to the regular decomposition. By minimality, the element a^485 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 638-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
